<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out $\dfrac{1}{4}$ of $48$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \ \text{of} \ 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {48} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 12
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$12$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \ \text{of} \ 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {48} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 12
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out $\dfrac{1}{2}$ of $\dfrac{1}{3}$ of $\dfrac{1}{4}$ of $48$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \text { of } \dfrac {1} {3} \text { of } \dfrac {1} {4} \text { of } 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {1} {3} \times \dfrac {1} {4} \times 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {48} {2 \times 3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 4 \times 2} {2 \times 3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {3} \times \cancel {4} \times 2} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {3} \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \text { of } \dfrac {1} {3} \text { of } \dfrac {1} {4} \text { of } 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {1} {3} \times \dfrac {1} {4} \times 48 \\\\
&= \dfrac {48} {2 \times 3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 4 \times 2} {2 \times 3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {3} \times \cancel {4} \times 2} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {3} \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2$
</code></pre>
</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

By how much is four and a half greater than three and seven eights? Write your answer as a fraction. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \dfrac {1} {2} - 3 \dfrac {7} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9} {2} - \dfrac {31} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 4} {2 \times 4} - \dfrac {31} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {36} {8} - \dfrac {31} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {36 - 31} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {8}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {5} {8}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \dfrac {1} {2} - 3 \dfrac {7} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9} {2} - \dfrac {31} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 4} {2 \times 4} - \dfrac {31} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {36} {8} - \dfrac {31} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {36 - 31} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {8}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {5} {8}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

The diagram shows a design formed by drawing six lines in a regular hexagon. The lines divide each edge of the hexagon into three equal parts. What fraction of the hexagon is shaded?

![dulwich-college--11-plus--maths--9999--specimen-paper-g/section-1-question-18-00.png](/assets/dulwich-college--11-plus--maths--9999--specimen-paper-g/section-1-question-18-00.png "dulwich-college--11-plus--maths--9999--specimen-paper-g/section-1-question-18-00.png")

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {number of triangles in one hexagon} &= 6 \\\\
\text {total triangles}                    &= 6 \times 7 + 12 \\\\
                                           &= 42 + 12 \\\\
                                           &= 54 \\\\
\text {fraction of hexagon shaded}         &= \dfrac {12} {54} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac {2 \times 6} {6 \times 9} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel{6}} {\cancel{6} \times 9} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac {2} {9}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {2} {9}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {number of triangles in one hexagon} &= 6 \\\\
\text {total triangles}                    &= 6 \times 7 + 12 \\\\
                                           &= 42 + 12 \\\\
                                           &= 54 \\\\
\text {fraction of hexagon shaded}         &= \dfrac {12} {54} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac {2 \times 6} {6 \times 9} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel{6}} {\cancel{6} \times 9} \\\\
                                           &= \dfrac {2} {9}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

$\dfrac{3}{4}$ of $840$. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \text { of } 840 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 840 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 840} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 210 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 210 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 210 \\\\
&= 630
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

$630$

</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

What fraction is half-way between $2$ and $2.5$?

-  $\dfrac{7}{2}$
-  $\dfrac{7}{4}$
-  $\dfrac{9}{4}$
-  $\dfrac{11}{4}$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (2 + 2.5) \\\\
&= \dfrac {4.5} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {45} {2 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 5} {2 \times 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times \cancel{5} } {2 \times 2 \times \cancel{5} } \\\\
&= \dfrac {9} {4}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>C</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {9} {4}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (2 + 2.5) \\\\
&= \dfrac {4.5} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {45} {2 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times 5} {2 \times 2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9 \times \cancel{5} } {2 \times 2 \times \cancel{5} } \\\\
&= \dfrac {9} {4}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>C</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {9} {4}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Extra squares}           &= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 12 - 5 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 12} {4} - 5 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 3 \times 4} {4} - 5 \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 3 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} - 5 \\\\
                                &= 3 \times 3 - 5\\\\
                                &= 9 - 5 \\\\
                                &= 4
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

What is $3 \dfrac{1}{2} \div \dfrac{1}{4}$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Dividing fractions is the same as multiplying by the reciprocal (inverse) of the second fraction.


$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \dfrac {1}{2} \div \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7}{2} \times 4 \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 4}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 2 \times 2}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 2 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 7 \times 2 \\\\
&= 14
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$14$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_divide_by_fraction_note
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \dfrac {1}{2} \div \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7}{2} \times 4 \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 4}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 2 \times 2}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {7 \times 2 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 7 \times 2 \\\\
&= 14
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$14$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

Emma has a rectangular  card $6 \ \text{cm}$ by $9 \ \text{cm}$. She colours in a border of $1 \ \text{cm}$ around  the edge. The border is $1 \ \text{cm}$ thick throughout. What fraction of the area of the rectangle is coloured in?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing starts.*

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


*TODO:: Show the diagram with the inner and outer rectangle*

*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


ABBR: $\text{Area of rectangle} = \text{length} \times \text{width}$


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Fraction coloured}       &= \dfrac {\text {Area of outer rectangle} - \text {Area of inner rectangle}} {\text {Area of inner rectangle}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {6 \times 9 - 4 \times 7 } { 6 \times 9 } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {54 - 28} { 54} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {26} { 54} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2 \times 13} {2 \times 27} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 13} {\cancel{2} \times 27} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {13} {27}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {13} {27}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_add_drawing_starts
*TODO:: Show the diagram with the inner and outer rectangle*
rtq_abbr_md_add_drawing_ends
<emptyline>
rtq_abbr_katex_formula_area_rectangle_expanded
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Fraction coloured}       &= \dfrac {\text {Area of outer rectangle} - \text {Area of inner rectangle}} {\text {Area of inner rectangle}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {6 \times 9 - 4 \times 7 } { 6 \times 9 } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {54 - 28} { 54} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {26} { 54} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2 \times 13} {2 \times 27} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 13} {\cancel{2} \times 27} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {13} {27}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

What fraction is bigger: two thirds or three quarters?
You must justify your answer clearly. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let's make the denominator the same for both, so we can compare them.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {3}, \quad \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 4} {3 \times 4}, \quad \dfrac {3 \times 3} {4 \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8} {12}, \quad \dfrac {9} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8} {12}  <  \dfrac {9} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {3}  <  \dfrac {3} {4} 
\end{aligned}
$

As per the calculation above, three quarters is greater than two thirds.

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

three quarters

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let's make the denominator the same for both, so we can compare them.
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {3}, \quad \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 4} {3 \times 4}, \quad \dfrac {3 \times 3} {4 \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8} {12}, \quad \dfrac {9} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {8} {12}  <  \dfrac {9} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {3}  <  \dfrac {3} {4} 
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
As per the calculation above, three quarters is greater than two thirds.
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>three quarters
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

Calculate $4 \dfrac{1}{2} - 1 \dfrac{3}{4}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 4 \dfrac {1} {2} - 1 \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\

&= \dfrac {18 - 7} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {11} {4} \\\\
&= 2 \dfrac {3} {4}
\end{aligned}

<div class='question question'>

What fraction of this square is shaded in?

![forest-school--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-2/section-1-question-16-00.png](/assets/forest-school--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-2/section-1-question-16-00.png "forest-school--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-2/section-1-question-16-00.png") 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Shaded Portion} &= 1 - \dfrac{4}{12} \\\\
                      &= 1 - \dfrac{4}{4 \times 3} \\\\
                      &= 1 - \dfrac{\cancel{4}}{\cancel{4} \times 3} \\\\
                      &= 1 - \dfrac{1}{3} \\\\
                      &= \drac{3 - 1}{3} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac{2}{3}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac{2}{3}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$

                      &= 1 - \dfrac{\cancel{4}}{\cancel{4} \times 3} \\\\
                      &= 1 - \dfrac{1}{3} \\\\
                      &= \drac{3 - 1}{3} \\\\
                      &= \dfrac{2}{3}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Dividing fractions is the same as multiplying by the reciprocal (inverse) of the second fraction.


$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {5} \div  \dfrac {4} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {5} \times \dfrac {10} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 10} {5 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {20}} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
&=1
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

&= \dfrac {20} {20} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {20}} {\cancel {20}} \\\\
&=1
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

&= \dfrac {27} {24} + \dfrac {4} {24} \\\\
&= \dfrac {27 + 4} {24} \\\\
&= \dfrac {31} {24} \\\\
&= 1 \dfrac {7} {24}
\end{aligned}

\begin{aligned}

                        &= \dfrac {2} {8} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {2 \times 4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}  \times 4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1}{4}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

                        &= \dfrac {2} {8} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {2 \times 4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}  \times 4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1}{4}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

                    &= \dfrac {2 \times 3 \times 4 \times 25} {2 \times 4} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 3 \times \cancel {4} \times 25} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
                    &= 75 \%  
\end{aligned}
$

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$\dfrac{2}{3} + \dfrac{5}{6}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {3} + \dfrac {5} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {3 \times 2} + \dfrac {5} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {6} + \dfrac {5} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 + 5} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 3} {3 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 3} {\cancel {3} \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {2}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {3} {2}$

</div>

&= \dfrac {4} {6} + \dfrac {5} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 + 5} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 3} {3 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 3} {\cancel {3} \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {2}
\end {aligned}
$

&= \dfrac {10} {12} - \dfrac {9} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 - 9} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {12}
\end{aligned}

<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

Write down two fractions which are equivalent to $4$ where one of the numbers is twenty.                               5 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

When the numerator is 20

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {4} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 5} {5 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {25}
\end{aligned}
$

When the denominator is 20

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {4} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 4} {5 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {16} {20}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {20} {25}, \dfrac {16} {20}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>When the numerator is 20
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {4} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 5} {5 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {25}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>
When the denominator is 20
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {4} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 4} {5 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {16} {20}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {20} {25}, \dfrac {16} {20}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

Find $\dfrac{2}{5}$ of $30$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {5} \ \text{of} \ 30 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {5} \times 30 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 30} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 5 \times 6} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel {5} \times 6} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
&= 2 \times 6 \\\\
&= 12
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$12$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {5} \ \text{of} \ 30 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {5} \times 30 \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 30} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 5 \times 6} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel {5} \times 6} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
&= 2 \times 6 \\\\
&= 12
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

Find $\dfrac{1}{2}$ of $72$.

<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {of} \ 72 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 72 \\\\
&= \dfrac {72} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 36} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 36} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 36
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

<div class='answer'>

$36$

</div>
<div class='papername'>
<p>king-edwards-school--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-1:3:1</p>
</div>
<div class='rag'>
<p>Waffles Woof</p>

<div class='rag'>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

The figure shows two identical overlapping triangles. Four-ninths of **each** triangle is shaded.
What fraction of the **whole** figure is shaded?

![king-edwards-school--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-1/section-3-question-1-00.png](/assets/king-edwards-school--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-1/section-3-question-1-00.png "king-edwards-school--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-1/section-3-question-1-00.png")

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the area of one traingle $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of the figure}      &= a + a - \dfrac {4} {9} \times a \\\\
                                &= 2a - \dfrac {4a} {9} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2a \times 9} {9} - \dfrac {4a} {9} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {18a} {9} - \dfrac {4a} {9} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {18a - 4a} {9} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {14a} {9} \\\\
\text {Fraction shaded}         &= \dfrac {4a} {9} \div \dfrac {14a} {9} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4a} {9} \times \dfrac {9} {14a} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4a \times 9} {9 \times 14a} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2 \times 2  \times a \times 9} {9 \times 2 \times 7 \times a } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 2 \times \cancel{a} \times \cancel{9}} {\cancel{9} \times \cancel{2} \times 7 \times \cancel{a} } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2} {7}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {2} {7}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the area of one traingle $= a$
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Area of the figure}      &= a + a - \dfrac {4} {9} \times a \\\\
                                &= 2a - \dfrac {4a} {9} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2a \times 9} {9} - \dfrac {4a} {9} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {18a} {9} - \dfrac {4a} {9} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {18a - 4a} {9} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {14a} {9} \\\\
\text {Fraction shaded}         &= \dfrac {4a} {9} \div \dfrac {14a} {9} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4a} {9} \times \dfrac {9} {14a} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {4a \times 9} {9 \times 14a} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2 \times 2  \times a \times 9} {9 \times 2 \times 7 \times a } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 2 \times \cancel{a} \times \cancel{9}} {\cancel{9} \times \cancel{2} \times 7 \times \cancel{a} } \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {2} {7}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {2} {7}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Work out three-sevenths of $91$.

- $42$

- $39$

- $36$

- $26$

- $13$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \text{ of } 91 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \times 91 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 91} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 13 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 13 \times \cancel{7}} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 13 \\\\
&= 39
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>B</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$39$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \text{ of } 91 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {7} \times 91 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 91} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 13 \times 7} {7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 13 \times \cancel{7}} {\cancel{7}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 13 \\\\
&= 39
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>B</code></pre>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

What number is halfway between $4\dfrac{1}{2}$ and $7\dfrac{1}{2}$ ?

- $5\dfrac{1}{4}$

- $5\dfrac{1}{2}$

- $5\dfrac{3}{4}$

-  $6$

- $6\dfrac{1}{4}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \bigg(4 \dfrac {1} {2} + 7 \dfrac {1} {2} \bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \bigg(\dfrac {9} {2} + \dfrac {15} {2}\bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \bigg(\dfrac {9 + 15} {2}\bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {24} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {24} {2 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \times 2 \times 2} {2 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{2}} \\\\
&= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>D</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \bigg(4 \dfrac {1} {2} + 7 \dfrac {1} {2} \bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \bigg(\dfrac {9} {2} + \dfrac {15} {2}\bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \bigg(\dfrac {9 + 15} {2}\bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \dfrac {24} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {24} {2 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \times 2 \times 2} {2 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel{2} \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2} \times \cancel{2}} \\\\
&= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>D</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

What fraction of this rectangle is shaded?

![latymer-upper-school--11-plus--maths--2014--sample-paper-1/section-1-question-12-00.png](/assets/latymer-upper-school--11-plus--maths--2014--sample-paper-1/section-1-question-12-00.png "latymer-upper-school--11-plus--maths--2014--sample-paper-1/section-1-question-12-00.png")

- $\dfrac{1}{10}$

- $\dfrac{3}{10}$

- $\dfrac{1}{3}$

- $\dfrac{1}{2}$

- ${2}\diagup{5}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {4} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2} \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {5} 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>E</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

${2}\diagup{5}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {4} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {2 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2} \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2} {5} 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>E</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>${2}\diagup{5}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Work out three eighths of $264$. 

- $61$ 

- $88$ 

- $96$ 

- $99$ 

- $108$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {8} \ \text {of} \ 264 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {8} \times 264 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 264} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 33 \times 8} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 33 \times \cancel {8}} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
&= 99
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='option'>
<p>D</p>
</div>
<div class='answer'>

$99$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {8} \ \text {of} \ 264 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {8} \times 264 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 264} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 33 \times 8} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 33 \times \cancel {8}} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
&= 99
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>D</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$99$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

Work out: $\dfrac{2}{5} - \dfrac{1}{3}$

- $\dfrac{3}{8}$

- $\dfrac{3}{15}$ 

- $\dfrac{1}{8}$ 

- $\dfrac{1}{15}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {5} - \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 3} {5 \times 3} - \dfrac {1 \times 5} {3 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6} {15} - \dfrac {5} {15} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 - 5} {15} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {15}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

$\dfrac {1} {15}$

\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {2} {5} - \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 3} {5 \times 3} - \dfrac {1 \times 5} {3 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6} {15} - \dfrac {5} {15} \\\\
&= \dfrac {6 - 5} {15} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {15}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>E</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {1} {15}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

&= \dfrac {3} {15} + \dfrac {10} {15} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 + 10} {15} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13} {15}
\end{aligned}
$

&= \dfrac {1 \times 3} {5 \times 3} + \dfrac {2 \times 5} {3 \times 5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {15} + \dfrac {10} {15} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 + 10} {15} \\\\
&= \dfrac {13} {15}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

In an orchestra of $24$ children, $\dfrac{3} {4}$ of them are girls. How many are boys?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Fraction of boys} &= 1 - \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {1} {4} \\\\
\text {Number of boys}   &= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 24 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {24} {4} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {6 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
                         &= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Fraction of boys} &= 1 - \dfrac {3} {4} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {1} {4} \\\\
\text {Number of boys}   &= \dfrac {1} {4} \times 24 \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {24} {4} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {6 \times 4} {4} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {6 \times \cancel{4}} {\cancel{4}} \\\\
                         &= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of fir trees} &= \dfrac {50} {100} \times 450 \\\\

                            &= \dfrac {5\cancel{0} \times 45\cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
                            &= 5 \times 45 \\\\
                            &= 225 
\end{aligned}
$

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Some of the fractions below are $\bold{smaller\:\:than\:\:\dfrac{1}{9}}$.

Tick them.

$\Box\:\:\dfrac{1}{10}\:\:\:\Box\:\:\dfrac{4}{9}\:\:\:\Box\:\:\dfrac{1}{2}\:\:\:\Box\:\:\dfrac{1}{100}\:\:\:\Box\:\:\dfrac{1}{8}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let's make the denominator the same for all the fractions.

LCM for $10, 9,  2, 100, 8 = 1800$

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{1}{9} , \quad \dfrac{1}{10} , \quad \dfrac{4}{9}, \quad \dfrac{1}{2}, \quad \dfrac{1}{100}, \quad \dfrac{1}{8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{1 \times 200}{9 \times 200} , \quad \dfrac{1 \times 180}{10 \times 180} , \quad \dfrac{4 \times 200}{9 \times 200}, \quad \dfrac{1 \times 900}{2 \times 900}, \quad \dfrac{1 \times 18}{100 \times 18}, \quad \dfrac{1 \times 225}{8 \times 225} \\\\
&= \dfrac{200}{1800} , \quad \dfrac{180}{1800}, \quad \dfrac{800}{1800}, \quad \dfrac{900}{1800}, \quad \dfrac{18}{1800}, \quad \dfrac{225}{1800}
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Fractions smaller} &= \dfrac{180}{1800}, \quad \dfrac{18}{1800} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {1} {10},   \quad  \dfrac {1} {100}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {10}$ 

</div>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {100}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let's make the denominator the same for all the fractions.
<emptyline>
LCM for $10, 9,  2, 100, 8 = 1800$

&= \dfrac{1 \times 200}{9 \times 200} , \quad \dfrac{1 \times 180}{10 \times 180} , \quad \dfrac{4 \times 200}{9 \times 200}, \quad \dfrac{1 \times 900}{2 \times 900}, \quad \dfrac{1 \times 18}{100 \times 18}, \quad \dfrac{1 \times 225}{8 \times 225} \\\\
&= \dfrac{200}{1800} , \quad \dfrac{180}{1800}, \quad \dfrac{800}{1800}, \quad \dfrac{900}{1800}, \quad \dfrac{18}{1800}, \quad \dfrac{225}{1800}
\end{aligned}
$
<emptyline>

\text{Fractions smaller} &= \dfrac{180}{1800}, \quad \dfrac{18}{1800} \\\\
                         &= \dfrac {1} {10},   \quad  \dfrac {1} {100}

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

In a magazine there are three adverts on the same page.

![leicester-high-school-for-girls--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-4/section-1-question-12-00.png](/assets/leicester-high-school-for-girls--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-4/section-1-question-12-00.png "leicester-high-school-for-girls--11-plus--maths--9999--sample-paper-4/section-1-question-12-00.png")

**In total**, what **fraction** of the page do three adverts use?

Show your working.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total fraction of the page used} &= \dfrac {1} {4} + \dfrac {1} {8} + \dfrac {1} {16} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {1 \times 4} {4 \times 4} + \dfrac {1 \times 2} {8 \times 2} + \dfrac {1 \times 1} {16 \times 1} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {4} {16} + \dfrac {2} {16} + \dfrac {1} {16} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {4 + 2 + 1} {16} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {7} {16}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {7} {16}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total fraction of the page used} &= \dfrac {1} {4} + \dfrac {1} {8} + \dfrac {1} {16} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {1 \times 4} {4 \times 4} + \dfrac {1 \times 2} {8 \times 2} + \dfrac {1 \times 1} {16 \times 1} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {4} {16} + \dfrac {2} {16} + \dfrac {1} {16} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {4 + 2 + 1} {16} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {7} {16}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {7} {16}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$\dfrac{5}{12} + \dfrac{4}{12}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {5} {12} + \dfrac {4} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 + 4} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 3} {3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 3} {\cancel {3} \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {3} {4}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {5} {12} + \dfrac {4} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 + 4} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9} {12} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 3} {3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 3} {\cancel {3} \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$\dfrac{1}{6} + \dfrac{1}{3}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {6} + \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {6} + \dfrac {1 \times 2} {3 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {6} + \dfrac {2} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 + 2} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {2 \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {3}} {2 \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {2}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {6} + \dfrac {1} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {6} + \dfrac {1 \times 2} {3 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {6} + \dfrac {2} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 + 2} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {6} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {2 \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {3}} {2 \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

Find $\dfrac{1}{3}$ of $2 \dfrac{1}{4}$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{1}{3} \ \text{of} \ 2 \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {3} \times 2 \dfrac {1} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {3} \times \dfrac {9} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9} {3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 3} {3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 3} {\cancel 3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {3} {4}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{1}{3} \ \text{of} \ 2 \dfrac{1}{4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {3} \times 2 \dfrac {1} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {3} \times \dfrac {9} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {9} {3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 3} {3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel 3 \times 3} {\cancel 3 \times 4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {3} {4}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

Which fraction is half way between $\dfrac{3}{7}$ and $\dfrac{4}{7}$?

Give your answer in its simplest form.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \bigg( \dfrac {3} {7} + \dfrac {4} {7} \bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \bigg( \dfrac {3 + 4} {7} \bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \bigg( \dfrac {7} {7} \bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 1 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {2}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \bigg( \dfrac {3} {7} + \dfrac {4} {7} \bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \bigg( \dfrac {3 + 4} {7} \bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \bigg( \dfrac {7} {7} \bigg) \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 1 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {1} {2}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

The contents of part of a wardrobe are shown in the pie chart below:

![merchant-taylors-school--11-plus--maths--9999--practice-paper-4/section-1-question-14-00.png](/assets/merchant-taylors-school--11-plus--maths--9999--practice-paper-4/section-1-question-14-00.png "merchant-taylors-school--11-plus--maths--9999--practice-paper-4/section-1-question-14-00.png")

What fraction, in its simplest form, of **Footwear** are boots? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Fraction of Boots}       &= \dfrac {\text {Boots}} {\text {Total Footwear}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {9} {63+9}    \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {9} {72}      \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{9}} {8 \times \cancel{9}}    \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1} {8}   
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {1} {8}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Fraction of Boots}       &= \dfrac {\text {Boots}} {\text {Total Footwear}} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {9} {63+9}    \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {9} {72}      \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel{9}} {8 \times \cancel{9}}    \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {1} {8}   
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {1} {8}$
</code></pre>
</div>

<!-- - if !options[:questions_only] -->
<!-- = render :reviewsubtag, question: question -->
<div class='question question'>

What is three-quarters of seven eighths? 

Write your answer in words. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac{3} {4} \text{ of } \dfrac {7} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3} {4} \times \dfrac {7} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{3 \times 7}{4 \times 8} \\\\
&= \dfrac{21}{32} \\\\
&= \text{twenty one over thirty two}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

twenty-one over thirty-two

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$

&= \dfrac{3 \times 7}{4 \times 8} \\\\

&= \text{twenty one over thirty two}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
